remember, and the conversation is about a city that exists only at dawn, and in that city there are streets that rearrange themselves when no one is looking, and the people who live there have learned to walk without expecting the same route twice, and they carry maps that are blank except for a single dot that moves, and the dot is always just ahead of them, and they follow it because following is
